# AI Growth Engine: Employee Sales & Marketing Automation
## Application Blueprint: PART 6 (Batch 6.6), Requirements Traceability Matrix

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Document | Application Blueprint, Part 6 (Batch 6.6): the **requirement-to-test traceability matrix (RTM)** for the whole product. It traces business requirements (BR, BR-S) → functional requirements (FR) → user stories (US) and acceptance criteria (AC) → test cases (TC, TC-AI, TC-AU, TC-NFR, TC-FLT, TC-BRS) and, for statements that are not software behaviour, verification items (VER); reports coverage % per level and per epic, distinguishing coverage by **test case** from coverage by **verification item**; traces non-functional requirements (NFR), business rules (BRL), AI agents (AG) and automations (AU) to tests; since version 1.2 also traces the **source-pack enhancements** (FR-ENH, NFR-ENH, enhancement stories and AC, TC-ENH, LT-ENH, OKPI-01…35, AG-037/038, AU-044…052) and the **source-pack aliases** (799 source tests, 302 SIT aliases, 321 source API operations, 245 QCs) to blueprint tests in sections (h), (i), (j); and flags every uncovered item with a remediation. Generated by `assembly/build_rtm.py` (with `assembly/rtm_enh.py`) from the Markdown of every Part and the coverage CSVs in `final/coverage/`; the CSV export is `final/RTM.csv` |
| Version | 1.2 · 27 Nov 2026 (extended with the source-pack levels h, i, j and regenerated after Batch 6.10; sections §6.6.1–§6.6.8 and their figures unchanged) |
| Baseline | Part 1 §1.12 (71 primary BR, 140 BR-S); Part 2.1 §2.4: **181 FRs = 150 interface/operation FRs + 31 cross-cutting FRs** (errata 82; the register's stated 33 cross-cutting / 183 total is superseded); Part 2.3 §2.25: **161 business rules** (errata 81; the stated 162 is superseded); Part 4.2 §4.22–§4.23 (NFR-001…099 with test methods); Part 5.1–5.3 (US and Gherkin AC); Part 6.1 (conventions §6.1.12, traceability specification §6.1.12.6, non-functional plans §6.1.14; owner of LT-01…LT-12 per errata 83); Batches 6.2a/6.2b/6.3a/6.3b/6.4/6.5 (test cases); Batch 6.7 (TC-NFR, TC-FLT, TC-BRS, VER); Part 7.1 (AG-001…036, AU-001…043); source-pack enhancement Parts 2.6, 2.7a/b, 3.4, 3.5a/b, 3.6a/b, 4.4a/b, 6.8a/b/c, 6.9a/b, 7.4a/b, 8.3a/b/c and closure Batch 6.10; `final/coverage/` CSVs; DEC-017 (TC IDs and SM-* aliases), DEC-014 (AP-08 semantic approval), BRD §12.1 (no required FR without a tested result) |
| Roles applied | QA Lead (primary), Business Systems Analyst, Product Owner (AP-08 approver), Principal Engineer, Security/Compliance/AI Governance Lead, DevOps/SRE Lead, FinOps Lead (budget verification items) |
| Tag ranges used | GAP-172 · RECOMMENDATION-080 issued in this file; ASSUMPTION-510…514 and GAP-510…514 issued in Batch 6.10 and used here; ASSUMPTION-194…196 and GAP-170…171 issued in Batch 6.5; ASSUMPTION-215…218 and RECOMMENDATION-090 issued in Batch 6.7 and used here |

**Acronyms used in this batch.** AC acceptance criterion · AG AI agent (Part 7) · OKPI optimization KPI of the source pack (document 38 KPI-01…35, renamed to avoid KPI-01…31) · QC quality control (source document 23) · SIT system integration test · AP acceptance procedure · AU automation (Part 7) · BR business requirement · BR-S supplementary business requirement · BRL business rule · BVA boundary value analysis · CI continuous integration · CSV comma-separated values · DT decision table · EVR evidence record · FR functional requirement · NFR non-functional requirement · RTM requirements traceability matrix · TC test case · TS test scenario · US user story · VER verification item.

**Time-sensitive facts.** This matrix is a design-time trace: it proves that every requirement has at least one *designed* test case or verification item. Execution results (Pass/Fail/Pending with EVR and build digest, Part 6.1 §6.1.12.6) are added by the CI run of the same script against test markers and VER evidence records once the code exists (RECOMMENDATION-080, RECOMMENDATION-090). No production certification is implied (BRD §13.2; Part 6.1 §6.1.5.5). Tool versions referenced: verify at time of implementation (as of 27 Nov 2026).

## 6.6.1 Method

### 6.6.1.1 How the matrix is built

The matrix is **generated, not hand-maintained** [RECOMMENDATION-080]. `assembly/build_rtm.py` parses the Markdown sources with regular expressions and computes every table in §6.6.2–§6.6.8 and `final/RTM.csv`:

| Step | Source and rule |
|---|---|
| 1 Requirements | Primary BR rows of Part 1 §1.12.1 (`BR-M##`, `BR-TENANT/WEB/AUTO/SVC/QA`) and BR-S rows of §1.12.2; FR rows of Part 2.1 §2.4 (`FR-W##`, `FR-A###`, `FR-OP###`; cross-cutting `FR-FLT/STD/DM/UIS/ENG/PUB/ADP/CSM/COP/SRCH/NEG`) with their BR traces, wave and epic; BRL rows of Part 2.3 §2.25; NFR rows of Part 4.2 with their test method; AG and AU rows of Part 7.1 |
| 2 Stories | Every `US-####` heading in Part 5.1 (files 1b, 1c), 5.2 and 5.3, with the IDs in its *Traces* / *Links* / *Linked* row (ranges such as `BRL-ELG-01…18` and `FR-A065–A072` expanded) and its `AC-####-##` scenarios |
| 3 Test cases | Every table row whose first cell is `TC-##-###`, `TC-AI-AG###-###` or `TC-AU-AU###-###` in Batches 6.2–6.5, and `TC-NFR-###`, `TC-FLT-###` or `TC-BRS-###` in Batch 6.7. Links are taken from the column whose header contains "Link" (Batch 6.7 uses "Linked"); AC shorthand (`AC-1602-04, -05`) and ranges are expanded. Coverage tables (rows starting with an FR/BR/BRL/NFR/US/AC ID that list TC IDs or TC ranges such as `TC-08-001…025`) add links; a range only resolves to TC IDs that are defined somewhere |
| 4 Verification items | Every row whose first cell is `VER-###` in Batch 6.7; requirement links from its "Linked" column; method (inspection, review, analysis, demonstration), owner and wave from the named columns. VER items are **not** test cases: they are counted in their own column and never raise the test-case count |
| 5 Derived links | AC → its story; TC → story via AC; FR → TC = explicit FR link **or** any TC of a story whose links include the FR; BR → TC = explicit BR link, via its FRs (Part 2.1 traces) or via stories that trace to it; BRL and NFR → TC = the rule/NFR ID appears anywhere in the TC row; AG/AU → TC from the TC-AI/TC-AU identifier; BR-S and NFR → VER from the VER "Linked" column |
| 6 Flags | *Covered* (≥ 1 TC or ≥ 1 VER), with the basis *Test case*, *Verification item* or *Test case + VER*; *Not covered* otherwise. Execution status is outside this design-time build |
| 7 Outputs | This document (tables marked "generated") and `final/RTM.csv` (columns: requirement_type, requirement_id, title, epic, wave, linked_fr, linked_us, aliases, tc_count, tc_ids, coverage_flag, ver_count, ver_ids, coverage_basis, and since v1.2 **api_ids, sit_aliases** per DEC-217 [ASSUMPTION-512]). Since v1.2 the CSV also has one row per source-pack item: FR-ENH, US-ENH, AC-ENH, NFR-ENH, LT-ENH, OKPI, AG/AU, SM-UT/SM-SIT/SM-UAT, SIT-ALIAS, SRC-API, QC, every TC-ENH (A…K, merged per DEC-217) and VER-ENH-K |
| 8 Source-pack layer (v1.2) | `rtm_enh.py` parses **every** `Part-*.md` (except the generated Parts 6.6, 9.2 and 9.3). A table row belongs to the IDs in its first cell (in grid tables every TC cell; in family-keyed rows such as `N01 \| TC-ENH-D-N01` the TC in the second cell); every other ID in the row is a link, with range and list expansion (`TC-ENH-B-011…014`, `TC-ENH-F-007, 008`, `FR-ENH-I-001…003`). Definitions: FR-ENH, NFR-ENH, LT-ENH, OKPI, AG/AU and TC-ENH rows whose first cell is the ID; stories from `US-<EP>-E<G><nn>` headings, story tables and Gherkin `Feature:` lines, with their `AC-…-E…` scenarios. Derived links: story → tests of the FR-ENH it realizes; FR-ENH → tests citing a control ID named in its requirement (RGV-, SMK-, AL-, ACC-, SEC-ROT, RBK-) [ASSUMPTION-510]; NFR-ENH and AG/AU → tests of the FR-ENH they name; OKPI → TC-NFR/VER of its NFRs and tests of its NFR-ENH; QC → tests and VER of the requirements and controls (AP-, DEC-, CA-, GL-, EG-, BLK-, RB-, ABAC-) in its `blueprint_verification_refs` [ASSUMPTION-511]. Aliases from the CSVs: `G_test_mapping.csv` (799 source tests → primary/supporting/TC-ENH tests), `H_sit_alias_map.csv` (SIT alias rule of §6.6.4), `D_api_mapping.csv` (source operation → TC-ENH-D test), `H_qc_map.csv` (QC). A referenced test counts only when it is defined somewhere in the blueprint |

Re-run: `python3 assembly/build_rtm.py` from the blueprint folder (writes this file and the CSV); `--check` prints the summary only. The FRD aliases are computed per DEC-017 and Part 6.1 §6.1.12.5 (SM-UAT-S001–S030 = FR-W01…W30, S031–S138 = FR-A001…A108; SM-UX-UAT per screen).

### 6.6.3.1 Supplementary business requirements BR-S001…S140 → TC / VER (generated)

A BR-S is covered by a **test case** when a TC reaches it (directly, through its traced FRs and stories, or through a TC-BRS of Part 6.7 §6.7.5.2), and by a **verification item** when a VER of Part 6.7 §6.7.5.3 links it. Statements that are not software behaviour (budget lines, effort ranges, team and timeline, phase gates, procurement, business goal) are verified by VER only: budget lines by FinOps review at the go/no-go gate, phase gates by the release readiness review at the wave gate (Part 6.7 §6.7.1.3, ASSUMPTION-217). 140 of 140 covered: 116 of 140 by test case, 24 by verification item only.

### 6.6.8.2 Semantic approval (AP-08)

**Amended by DEC-203 (Part 9.5):** 100% of requirement-to-test links are individually approved (OKPI-20, tiers T-A/T-B/T-C of Part 6.9b §6.9.5.3.5); the 10% spot check described below is kept only as a QA sampling audit. The mechanical matrix proves that a link exists, not that the test is the right test. AP-08 (DEC-014; Part 6.1 §6.1.13.6) requires the Product Owner to approve the requirement → test mapping for each release scope after a **10% spot check** of links for semantic correctness. The spot-check sample is drawn by the QA Lead from `final/RTM.csv` (stratified: every P0 risk area RA-01…RA-20 and RA-26, at least 5 FRs per epic in scope, and at least 5 VER items per release, checking that the method, owner and evidence fit the statement), and the result is recorded as an EVR with business acceptance.
